# Daily video prompt pack — 14/11/2026 · Dragon day (ថ្ងៃឆ្នាំរោង)

Lucky signs: Dragon, Rat, Monkey · Careful: Dog
Day element: Yang Water 💧 (Ren-chen day; ធាតុទឹក) · every action scene today carries Water magic: flowing ribbons of clear blue water, rippling rings of light and cool silver-blue mist.
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky-numbers scene of about 10–12 seconds.
Two versions: make the pictures once, then record a Khmer voice-over and an English voice-over (two videos, or one video with both subtitle tracks).
Meaning scenes: in the love, work, money and horoscope scenes the zodiac guardian and its animal act out what Chanda is saying, the guardian protecting and caring and the animal trusting (no real-life people), and every scene with Chanda on camera says which feeling she shows.
Ratings, lucky signs and lucky numbers come from the site's fixed rules and match the website's daily fortune for this date.
Voice-over wording: standard script (this pack was made ahead; the site's own daily wording appears the day before).

## Today's horoscope – all 12 signs
Each sign's prediction for 14/11/2026: general luck, love, work, money and advice, in English and Khmer, ready to copy into your Facebook, TikTok, Telegram and YouTube posts. The videos speak the same predictions: the 60-second video (the Dragon's luck and love), the 180-second video (love, work and money), the long videos (every sign) and the full-fortune video. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs, as in the videos.
Wording: the website's standard lines for each sign's rating (this date's written fortune is published on the website the day before; open the Studio at mybirthsign.com/studio/ then to use it).

Money: A good day to review your budget and set a little aside.
Advice: Work together with a friend today; shared effort brings better results.

### Khmer
🐀 ឆ្នាំជូត – ថ្ងៃល្អ
ទូទៅ៖ ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។ សូមរក្សាស្នាមញញឹម។
ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។ គ្រាន់តែស្ដាប់ ក៏គ្រប់គ្រាន់ហើយ។
ការងារ៖ ការងារជាក្រុម ដំណើរការល្អ។ សូមតាមដានកិច្ចការ ឬការកុម្ម៉ង់ដែលនៅសល់ថ្ងៃនេះ។
លុយកាក់៖ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ពិនិត្យថវិកា ហើយសន្សំទុកបន្តិច។
ដំបូន្មាន៖ សូមធ្វើការជាមួយមិត្តភក្តិថ្ងៃនេះ ការខិតខំរួមគ្នា នាំមកនូវលទ្ធផលល្អជាង។

One hero post for the day's luckiest animal: a picture, a short video and a caption. Lucky color gold (មាស) · best time 15:00–17:00 · lucky numbers 1 · 11 · 664 · 6747 · 11217 (for fun only).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a photorealistic hero image of @Dragon (the Dragon guardian with their dragon), the Dragon of the Chinese zodiac, as a majestic celestial guardian from a high-budget fantasy epic. The dragon symbolizes power, good fortune and a noble spirit: a majestic, proud and noble expression and posture that show this, mouth calm and closed. Realistic anatomy and textures, engraved gold ornaments with physically real metallic reflections. It stands in a courtyard of a temple inspired by Angkor Wat, beside two carved stone lions and a row of glowing oil lamps, under an enormous luminous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky; moonbeams fall on the dragon, golden particles and flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light swirl around it. Cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, volumetric mist, cinematic depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around it. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cartoon or plastic look, no cropped body parts.

Today's luckiest zodiac animal, the Dragon, in 80 seconds. For viewers who follow Chinese zodiac signs, especially people born in the Year of the Dragon, scrolling Facebook Reels and TikTok.

### Scene 8 – Goodbye – Chanda on camera (1:10–1:20)
Two-shot of Chanda and the dragon together on the moonlit temple terrace, both clearly in frame. @Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, faces the camera, smiling and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements. As she talks she opens her palm, and a small golden lotus blooms in her hand, glows brighter and bursts into thousands of golden sparkles that swirl in a slow magical spiral around her and @Dragon (the Dragon guardian with their dragon), right beside her. The dragon looks up at the magic in wonder and reacts with happy, lively body language, then waves goodbye together with Chanda without speaking, its mouth closed. Long golden ribbons of light unfurl from the temple towers and wave across the starry sky. Crane shot slowly pulling back and rising for a warm final view, with open sky at the top for a title. Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
14 November belongs to the Dragon. Year of the Dragon, this Great Day is yours! A Yang Water day!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Ruler of the clouds and rain, the Dragon symbolizes power, good fortune, and a noble spirit. Let that spirit guide you today.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
This is your day. Love: A good day to reach out, share a meal and show your affection openly. Trust yourself.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Your lucky color today is gold. Wear it, or carry something gold with you. Let it remind you to smile and stay confident.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Lucky direction: the southwest. Best time: from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. Face that way when you make a wish.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Your lucky numbers: 1, 11, and 664. Comment your sign below, and save this video!

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Come back tomorrow for a brand-new fortune. Follow MyBirthSign, and have a lucky day!

## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Dragon, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Dragon as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Dragon's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Dragon and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Daily fortune
   English: Born in the Year of the Dragon? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោងមែនទេ? នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។
2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
3. Personality
   English: If you were born in the Year of the Dragon, people may describe you as confident, charismatic, and ambitious. Today, that side of you shines.
   Khmer: បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន ប្រហែលជាស្គាល់អ្នកថា ជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ទាក់ទាញចំណាប់អារម្មណ៍ និងមានមហិច្ឆតា។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ចំណុចល្អនោះកំពុងភ្លឺ។
4. Challenge
   English: Can you guess today's luckiest zodiac animal? Three clues: confident, charismatic, and ambitious.
   Khmer: ទាយមើល៖ តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ? តម្រុយ៖ ជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ទាក់ទាញចំណាប់អារម្មណ៍ និងមានមហិច្ឆតា។
5. Symbol

## Voice-over – 180-second video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Dragon? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Saturday, 14 November 2026, is a Dragon day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Dragon. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Ruler of the clouds and rain, the Dragon symbolizes power, good fortune, and a noble spirit. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Dragon are confident, charismatic, and ambitious. Dragons draw attention and often rise to leadership naturally.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Dragon? Your luck is strong today. Stay majestic, proud and noble. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Love and family today: A good day to reach out, share a meal and show your affection openly.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Work today: Customers and coworkers respond well to you. A good day to present your ideas and close a deal.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Money today: Good for fair deals and collecting what is owed. Keep your records neat.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is gold. Wear something gold, or carry a small gold item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the southwest. When you start something new, face the southwest, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 1, 11, and 664. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 1, 11, 664!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Dragon's lucky days of the week are Tuesday and Saturday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Dragon can come across as proud or impatient with limitations. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: gold. Best time: from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. Lucky direction: southwest. Lucky numbers: 1, 11, and 664.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

00:00:02,442 --> 00:00:07,392
នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ

3
00:00:07,442 --> 00:00:09,950
ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។

4
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:12,528
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃសៅរ៍ ទី 14 ខែវិច្ឆិកា

5
00:00:12,578 --> 00:00:14,716
ឆ្នាំ 2026 គឺជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំរោង។

6
00:00:14,766 --> 00:00:17,997
នេះជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង។

7
00:00:18,047 --> 00:00:19,950
ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក!

8
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,408
នាគ គឺជាម្ចាស់នៃពពក និងភ្លៀង។

9
00:00:22,458 --> 00:00:24,187
ឆ្នាំរោងតំណាងឲ្យអំណាច

10
00:00:24,237 --> 00:00:26,475
សំណាងល្អ និងចិត្តថ្លៃថ្នូរ។

11
00:00:26,525 --> 00:00:29,950
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថាមពលល្អទាំងនេះ កំពុងនៅជាមួយអ្នក។

12
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:32,905
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង ជាមនុស្សជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង

13
00:00:32,955 --> 00:00:35,708
ទាក់ទាញចំណាប់អារម្មណ៍ និងមានមហិច្ឆតា។

14
00:00:35,758 --> 00:00:37,677
រោងជាអ្នកដឹកនាំដោយធម្មជាតិ

15
00:00:37,727 --> 00:00:39,950
ហើយតែងទទួលបានការគោរពពីអ្នកដទៃ។

16
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,172
សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង ថ្ងៃនេះ

17
00:00:42,222 --> 00:00:44,172
សំណាងរបស់អ្នកល្អខ្លាំងណាស់។

18
00:00:44,222 --> 00:00:46,839
សូមរក្សាចិត្តឲ្យស្ងប់ ទទួលយកឱកាសល្អៗ

19
00:00:46,889 --> 00:00:49,950
ហើយចែករំលែកភាពរីករាយ ដល់មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន។

20
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,450
ស្នេហា និងគ្រួសារថ្ងៃនេះ៖

21
00:00:52,500 --> 00:00:56,950
ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ទាក់ទងគ្នា ញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា

22
00:00:57,000 --> 00:00:59,950
និងបង្ហាញក្ដីស្រឡាញ់ដោយបើកចំហ។

23
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,987
ការងារថ្ងៃនេះ៖ អតិថិជន

24
00:01:02,037 --> 00:01:05,506
និងមិត្តរួមការងារ ឆ្លើយតបល្អចំពោះអ្នក។

25
00:01:05,556 --> 00:01:08,006
ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់បង្ហាញគំនិត

26
00:01:08,056 --> 00:01:09,950
និងបិទកិច្ចព្រមព្រៀង។

27
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:11,450
លុយកាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ៖

28
00:01:11,500 --> 00:01:14,750
ល្អសម្រាប់កិច្ចព្រមព្រៀងយុត្តិធម៌

29
00:01:14,800 --> 00:01:17,450
និងប្រមូលប្រាក់ដែលគេជំពាក់។

30
00:01:17,500 --> 00:01:19,950
សូមរក្សាបញ្ជីឲ្យបានស្អាត។

31
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,923
ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌មាស។

32
00:01:22,973 --> 00:01:26,076
សូមស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌មាស ឬយករបស់ពណ៌មាសមួយ

33
00:01:26,126 --> 00:01:29,950
ជាប់ខ្លួន ដើម្បីរក្សាសំណាងល្អ ឲ្យនៅជិតអ្នក។

34
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,251
ពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ

35
00:01:32,301 --> 00:01:34,906
គឺម៉ោង 3 រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ល្ងាច។

36
00:01:34,956 --> 00:01:37,472
ជាពេលល្អ សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ

37
00:01:37,522 --> 00:01:39,950
ការជួបជុំ ឬការចាប់ផ្ដើមថ្មី។

38
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,867
ទិសសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺទិសនិរតី។

39
00:01:42,917 --> 00:01:46,533
ពេលចាប់ផ្ដើមធ្វើអ្វីថ្មី សូមបែរមុខទៅទិសនិរតី

40
00:01:46,583 --> 00:01:49,950
ហើយបោះជំហានទៅមុខ ដោយចិត្តស្ងប់ និងរីករាយ។

41
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:53,677
សម្រាប់លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 1 លេខ 11 និងលេខ 664។

42
00:01:53,727 --> 00:01:57,314
សូមសរសេរលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក នៅក្នុងមតិយោបល់

43
00:01:57,364 --> 00:01:59,950
ហើយតោះនិយាយម្ដងទៀត៖ 1 11 664!

44
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:03,161
ថ្ងៃសំណាងប្រចាំសប្ដាហ៍ របស់ឆ្នាំរោង

45
00:02:03,211 --> 00:02:05,455
គឺថ្ងៃអង្គារ និងថ្ងៃសៅរ៍។

46
00:02:05,505 --> 00:02:07,198
សូមចងចាំថ្ងៃទាំងនេះ

47
00:02:07,248 --> 00:02:09,950
សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ របស់អ្នក។

48
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:12,533
សូមរំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង

49
00:02:12,583 --> 00:02:16,117
ជួនកាលមើលទៅអំនួត ឬអត់ធ្មត់ទាបចំពោះដែនកំណត់។

50
00:02:16,167 --> 00:02:18,117
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព

51
00:02:18,167 --> 00:02:19,950
និងស្ដាប់ចិត្តខ្លួនឯង។

52
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:22,514
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមប្រាថ្នាអ្វីមួយតូចៗ

53
00:02:22,564 --> 00:02:26,446
ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។

54
00:02:26,496 --> 00:02:29,950
ចិត្តដែលស្ងប់ នឹងជួយនាំសំណាងល្អ មករកអ្នក។

55
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:31,306
មុនថ្ងៃនេះបញ្ចប់

56
00:02:31,356 --> 00:02:34,526
សូមចំណាយពេលស្ងាត់មួយភ្លែត ដើម្បីអរគុណ។

57
00:02:34,576 --> 00:02:37,662
អរគុណមនុស្សដែលជួយអ្នក ហើយអរគុណខ្លួនឯង

58
00:02:37,712 --> 00:02:39,950
ដែលបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

59
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,604
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

60
00:02:41,654 --> 00:02:42,942
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌មាស។

61
00:02:42,992 --> 00:02:45,777
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 3 រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ល្ងាច។

62
00:02:45,827 --> 00:02:47,273
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។

63
00:02:47,323 --> 00:02:49,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 1 លេខ 11 និងលេខ 664។

64
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

65
00:02:52,308 --> 00:02:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

66
00:02:54,423 --> 00:02:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

67
00:02:56,282 --> 00:02:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

68
00:02:58,590 --> 00:02:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

## Long YouTube video – all 12 signs (about 5 minutes)
A long YouTube video (5:10): today's luckiest animal, an overview, then all 12 signs in order (two 10-second clips each), then a short closing. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each sign's clips name its own character). Chanda opens the video on camera and narrates the rest off screen; the animals never speak. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 31.

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-14-long.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:03,243

2
00:00:03,293 --> 00:00:06,657
and one should take it easy.

3

Where does your sign stand?

4
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:12,376
Today, Saturday, 14 November 2026

5
00:00:12,426 --> 00:00:13,553
is a Dragon day.

6
00:00:13,603 --> 00:00:16,126
It is a Great Day for everyone born

7
00:00:16,176 --> 00:00:18,038
in the Year of the Dragon.

8

9
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,208

10

the Dragon symbolizes power

11
00:00:24,435 --> 00:00:27,047
good fortune, and a noble spirit.

12

14

15

16

17

18

19

time is from 3 to 5 in the afternoon.

Love: Spend a little quality time

22
00:00:53,143 --> 00:00:54,902
with someone close.

23
00:00:54,952 --> 00:00:57,474
Lucky direction: southwest.

24
00:00:57,524 --> 00:00:59,950
Lucky numbers: 33 and 215.

25
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,095
Year of the Ox.

26
00:01:01,145 --> 00:01:04,225
Today is an ordinary, steady day for you.

27
00:01:04,275 --> 00:01:07,278

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden dragon wake the night flowers before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the flowers fell into a deep sleep. Nothing could wake them. Without their glow, the festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden dragon. Today was a Dragon day, and the dragon felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A little bird named Dara flew down. "Little Dragon," she said, "only you can wake the night flowers. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Ruler of the clouds and rain, the Dragon symbolizes power, good fortune, and a noble spirit. Dara smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To wake the night flowers, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the dragon, and it set off on its adventure, with Dara right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Dragon are confident, charismatic, and ambitious. Dragons draw attention and often rise to leadership naturally.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was gold, the dragon's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The dragon tied a gold ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Dara, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the southwest, the dragon's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But the path climbed a steep stone hill, with a strong wind blowing. The dragon stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Dragon can come across as proud or impatient with limitations.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Dara came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," she said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Step by step, the dragon climbed the hill. The wind grew softer, and at the top, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Dara. For the Dragon today, that is from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the dragon rang the golden bells once, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The dragon climbed 11 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found a single drop of golden dew, sparkling in a crystal shell. It was the magic that wakes the flowers!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the dew drop, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Tuesday and Saturday shone brightest, the Dragon's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the dew drop carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Dara right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The dragon let the golden dew fall on the flowers. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, one by one, the flowers opened and glowed!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The night flowers glowed again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The dragon felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Dragon, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: gold. Best time: from 3 to 5 in the afternoon. Lucky direction: southwest. Lucky numbers: 1, 11, and 664.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Dara fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

គ្មានអ្វីអាចដាស់វាបានទេ។

8
00:00:16,098 --> 00:00:17,511
បើគ្មានពន្លឺរបស់វា

9

ពិធីបុណ្យមិនអាចចាប់ផ្ដើមបានទេ។

10

នៅកណ្ដាលស្រះ ផ្កាឈូកមាសមួយបានរីក

11

ហើយមាននាគមាសតូចមួយ ចេញមក។

12

ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំរោង

13

ហើយនាគមានអារម្មណ៍ពិសេសណាស់។

14

កូនបក្សីតូចមួយ ឈ្មោះតារា ហោះចុះមក។

15
00:00:32,636 --> 00:00:34,291
តារានិយាយថា "នាគតូចអើយ

16
00:00:34,341 --> 00:00:36,849
មានតែអ្នកទេ ដែលអាចដាស់ផ្កាពេលយប់។

17
00:00:36,899 --> 00:00:39,020
ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់អ្នក!

18
00:00:39,070 --> 00:00:39,950
"

19
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,216
នាគ គឺជាម្ចាស់នៃពពក និងភ្លៀង។

20
00:00:42,266 --> 00:00:43,856
ឆ្នាំរោងតំណាងឲ្យអំណាច

21
00:00:43,906 --> 00:00:45,966
សំណាងល្អ និងចិត្តថ្លៃថ្នូរ។

22
00:00:46,016 --> 00:00:46,903
តារាញញឹម។

23
00:00:46,953 --> 00:00:49,950
នេះហើយ ជាហេតុដែលព្រះចន្ទ បានជ្រើសរើសវា។

24
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:53,394
ដើម្បីដាស់ផ្កាពេលយប់ វីរបុរសតូច

25
00:00:53,444 --> 00:00:56,394
ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖

26
00:00:56,444 --> 00:00:59,950
ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

27
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,350
នាគនិយាយថា "តោះទៅ!

28
00:01:02,400 --> 00:01:05,817
" ហើយវាក៏ចេញដំណើរផ្សងព្រេង

29
00:01:05,867 --> 00:01:09,950
ដោយមានតារា នៅក្បែរខ្លួនជានិច្ច។

30

អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង ជាមនុស្សជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង

31
00:01:12,955 --> 00:01:15,708
ទាក់ទាញចំណាប់អារម្មណ៍ និងមានមហិច្ឆតា។

32
00:01:15,758 --> 00:01:17,677
រោងជាអ្នកដឹកនាំដោយធម្មជាតិ

33
00:01:17,727 --> 00:01:19,950
ហើយតែងទទួលបានការគោរពពីអ្នកដទៃ។

34
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,264
រតនសម្បត្តិទីមួយ កំពុងរង់ចាំ

35
00:01:22,314 --> 00:01:24,248
នៅក្នុងសួនចង្កៀងមួយពាន់។

36
00:01:24,298 --> 00:01:26,727
មានតែមួយប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលភ្លឺជាងគេ។

37
00:01:26,777 --> 00:01:29,950
វាគឺពណ៌មាស ដែលជាពណ៌សំណាងរបស់នាគថ្ងៃនេះ។

38
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,524
នាគចងខ្សែបូពណ៌មាស ជុំវិញក។

39
00:01:32,574 --> 00:01:36,287
តារានិយាយថា "ស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នក

40
00:01:36,337 --> 00:01:38,762
ហើយសំណាងល្អ នឹងនៅជិតអ្នក។

41
00:01:38,812 --> 00:01:39,950
"

42
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,820
បន្ទាប់មក ពួកគេមកដល់ផ្លូវបំបែកមួយ

43
00:01:42,870 --> 00:01:44,472
ដែលមានផ្លូវជាច្រើន។

44
00:01:44,522 --> 00:01:46,472
ផ្លូវមួយ ចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

45
00:01:46,522 --> 00:01:49,950
វាចង្អុលទៅទិសនិរតី ដែលជាទិសសំណាងរបស់នាគ។

46
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:52,491
ប៉ុន្តែផ្លូវនោះ ឡើងភ្នំថ្មដ៏ចោត

47
00:01:52,541 --> 00:01:54,212
ហើយមានខ្យល់បក់ខ្លាំង។

48
00:01:54,262 --> 00:01:55,196
នាគឈប់។

49
00:01:55,246 --> 00:01:57,819
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង ជួនកាលមើលទៅអំនួត

50
00:01:57,869 --> 00:01:59,950
ឬអត់ធ្មត់ទាបចំពោះដែនកំណត់។

51
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:01,715
តារាមកជិត ហើយញញឹម។

52
00:02:01,765 --> 00:02:04,264
តារានិយាយថា "ដកដង្ហើមយឺតៗ។

53
00:02:04,314 --> 00:02:06,028
ជឿលើចិត្តរបស់អ្នក។

54
00:02:06,078 --> 00:02:08,774
អ្នកខ្លាំងជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។

55
00:02:08,824 --> 00:02:09,950
"

56
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:13,115
ម្ដងមួយជំហានៗ នាគឡើងភ្នំ។

57
00:02:13,165 --> 00:02:16,279
ខ្យល់ចាប់ផ្ដើមបក់ស្រាលវិញ

58
00:02:16,329 --> 00:02:19,950
ហើយនៅលើកំពូល វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

59
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,371
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា

60
00:02:23,421 --> 00:02:26,003
តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។

61
00:02:26,053 --> 00:02:29,950
តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

62
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,950
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ

63
00:02:33,000 --> 00:02:34,314
នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។

64
00:02:34,364 --> 00:02:37,223
នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង

65
00:02:37,273 --> 00:02:39,950
ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

66
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,591
តារានិយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ

67
00:02:41,641 --> 00:02:44,325
បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។

68
00:02:44,375 --> 00:02:45,809
" សម្រាប់នាគថ្ងៃនេះ

69
00:02:45,859 --> 00:02:48,153
គឺម៉ោង 3 រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ល្ងាច។

70
00:02:48,203 --> 00:02:49,950
ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

71
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,746
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង នាគវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស

72
00:02:52,796 --> 00:02:56,617
ចំនួន 1 ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។

73
00:02:56,667 --> 00:02:57,907
ទីង!

74
00:02:57,957 --> 00:02:59,950
ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

75
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:03,283
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។

76
00:03:03,333 --> 00:03:05,896
នាគឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួន 11 កាំ

77
00:03:05,946 --> 00:03:09,950
ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

78
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:11,405
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ

79
00:03:11,455 --> 00:03:14,677
ពួកគេបានរកឃើញដំណក់ទឹកសន្សើមពណ៌មាសមួយ

80
00:03:14,727 --> 00:03:17,586
ភ្លឺចែងចាំង ក្នុងសំបកគ្រីស្តាល់។

81
00:03:17,636 --> 00:03:19,950
វាគឺជាវេទមន្ត ដែលដាស់ផ្កា!

82
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:21,417
នៅជុំវិញដំណក់ទឹកសន្សើម

83
00:03:21,467 --> 00:03:23,817
មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ

84
00:03:23,867 --> 00:03:26,017
មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។

85
00:03:26,067 --> 00:03:28,150
ថ្ងៃអង្គារ និងថ្ងៃសៅរ៍ ភ្លឺជាងគេ

86
00:03:28,200 --> 00:03:29,950
ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំរោង។

87
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,524
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។

88
00:03:31,574 --> 00:03:35,783
វីរបុរសតូច កាន់ដំណក់ទឹកសន្សើមយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន

89
00:03:35,833 --> 00:03:39,950
ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានតារា នៅក្បែរ។

90
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,485
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។

91
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438
យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។

92
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

93
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,027
នាគទម្លាក់ទឹកសន្សើមមាស ទៅលើផ្កា។

94
00:03:53,077 --> 00:03:55,719
មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។

95
00:03:55,769 --> 00:03:59,950
បន្ទាប់មក ផ្កាម្ដងមួយៗ ក៏រីក ហើយភ្លឺចែងចាំង!

96
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:01,897
ផ្កាពេលយប់ ភ្លឺឡើងវិញ។

97
00:04:01,947 --> 00:04:03,136
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

98
00:04:03,186 --> 00:04:06,587
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

99
00:04:06,637 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

100
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,147
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

101
00:04:13,197 --> 00:04:15,032
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

102
00:04:15,082 --> 00:04:17,819
នាគមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

103
00:04:17,869 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

104
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,916
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោង ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

105
00:04:23,966 --> 00:04:26,588
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

106
00:04:26,638 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

107
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,604
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

108
00:04:31,654 --> 00:04:32,942
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌មាស។

109
00:04:32,992 --> 00:04:35,777
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 3 រសៀល ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ល្ងាច។

110
00:04:35,827 --> 00:04:37,273
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។

111
00:04:37,323 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 1 លេខ 11 និងលេខ 664។

112
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,107
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

113
00:04:44,157 --> 00:04:47,478
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងតារា ក៏លង់លក់

114

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Dog (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Dog, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Dog clashes with the Dragon day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Dog as ingredients (the Dog is the only zodiac character in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the dog hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little lotus lantern keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Stop the scroll
   English: Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Dog? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.
   Khmer: ឈប់! កុំអូសរំលង។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំច រាសីអ្នកថ្ងៃនេះមិនល្អទេ។ តែកុំបារម្ភ សូមមើលឲ្យចប់។
2. Warning
   English: Year of the Dog, wait! Today is a clash day for you. Here's how to get through it calmly.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំច ចាំបន្តិច! ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃប៉ះឆុងសម្រាប់អ្នក។ មកមើលវិធីឆ្លងកាត់ថ្ងៃនេះដោយស្ងប់។
★ 3. Question
   English: Does everything feel a bit off today? If you were born in the Year of the Dog, here's why.

4. Send it to a friend
   English: Know someone born in the Year of the Dog? Send this to them before they make a big decision today.
   Khmer: ស្គាល់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំចទេ? ផ្ញើវីដេអូនេះទៅគាត់ មុនពេលគាត់សម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។
5. Only one sign
   English: Out of all twelve signs, only one should slow down today: the Dog.

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands on the carved stone temple terrace above the moonlit lotus pond, facing the camera and speaking warmly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures. She starts by raising one open hand toward the camera like a friendly "stop" sign, with a caring, slightly urgent expression, then lowers it and keeps talking warmly. Below her, at the edge of the pond where a path of flat stepping stones leads up to the temple, @Dog (the Dog guardian with their dog) sits looking a little tired and lets out a quiet sigh with its mouth closed; it does not speak. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward Chanda. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda; the dog is the saved Flow character @Dog. Use the saved Flow character @Chanda exactly as she is: same face, hairstyle, outfit and jewelry in every scene. Her face shows quiet compassion: she holds one hand open toward the viewer as if offering comfort, and smiles reassuringly. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical. Sky: soft ribbons of gold-and-violet aurora wave slowly across the sky above the temple towers; it stays in the background and never covers a face.
English voice: Audio: Chanda's voice, identical in every clip of this series (the same woman, same timbre, same pitch, same accent, same speed, same energy): a young Cambodian woman in her mid-twenties speaking clear, natural English with a neutral accent, medium-high pitch, a soft, warm and bright tone, a gentle smile in her voice, the same calm, cheerful energy from the first word to the last (never louder, faster, deeper, older or more dramatic), a steady pace of about 150 words per minute, even volume, recorded close to a good studio microphone in a quiet room with no echo or reverb; no whispering, no singing, no other voices. Chanda is on camera and speaks to the viewer with natural lip movements that match her words. She says: "Does everything feel a bit off today? If you were born in the Year of the Dog, here's why." Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak.

### Scene 2 – A wobbly start (0:10–0:20)
Continuing from the previous scene: the dog jumps up and hurries across the flat stepping stones toward the glowing temple in the distance. Halfway across, a stone tips a little, the dog wobbles, catches its balance and stops, a bit surprised but not hurt. Camera follows behind it. Same moonlit lotus pond, same stepping-stone path and temple, same lighting and same character design as the previous scene. Natural, believable movement and body language, soft realistic moonlight, gentle pace. The dog moves like a real, living animal, never still like a statue: it breathes, blinks, and its ears, tail and body move naturally with its feelings; its mouth stays closed. ## Voice-over – take-it-easy video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Does everything feel a bit off today? If you were born in the Year of the Dog, here's why.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, 14 November, is a Dragon day, opposite the Dog in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Be careful today: you can be anxious or overly suspicious of new situations. Pause before you react. Small steps still count.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next. Take a deep breath first.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

## Long YouTube video – full fortune, 8:40 (mid-roll ads)
A full daily fortune for YouTube (8:40, 52 clips of 10 seconds), long enough for mid-roll ads. In order: the hook, the Great Day sign (Dragon) in depth, the two Good Day signs (Rat and Monkey), the sign to take it easy (Dog) with its little story, the other eight signs, then a recap and goodbye. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients in Flow (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each part names its own character). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 4 and 52; in the others she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. Set the clip length to 10 seconds, make the clips in order 1 to 52, and paste the chapters into the YouTube description.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Great Day: Dragon
2:40 Good Day: Rat
3:30 Good Day: Monkey
4:20 Take it easy: Dog
5:20 All the other signs
8:00 Recap and goodbye

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Every sign in one line
   English: One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានមួយឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ពីរឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ តើអ្នកជាឆ្នាំណា?
2. Before a big decision
   English: Before you make any big decision today, watch this: your lucky color, your best time, and whether to slow down.
   Khmer: មុននឹងសម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ពេលល្អ និងថាតើគួរធ្វើយឺតៗឬទេ។
3. Speak to the careful sign
   English: Born in the Year of the Dog? Watch this before you do anything big today. Everyone else, your luck is here too.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំចមែនទេ? សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ មុនធ្វើរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកផ្សេងទៀត សំណាងរបស់អ្នកក៏នៅទីនេះដែរ។
4. Name the winner
   English: Today the Dragon has its Great Day, and one sign should take it easy. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ ឆ្នាំរោងមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ហើយមានមួយឆ្នាំគួរប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ តើជាឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
★ 5. Comment first
   English: Comment your sign now. By the end of this video, you'll know your lucky color, best time, and direction for today.
   Khmer: សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នកឥឡូវនេះ។ មុនវីដេអូនេះចប់ អ្នកនឹងដឹងពណ៌ ពេល និងទិសសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ = used to open this video today (the hook changes from day to day, and each one gives every viewer a reason to stay to find their own sign). To use another hook, replace the voice line in scene 1.

### Mid-roll ads
YouTube lets you add ad breaks in the middle of videos that are 8 minutes or longer; this one is 8:40. Put the breaks at chapter starts so they don't cut a sentence. Chapter starts after 2:00: 2:40, 3:30, 4:20, 5:20, 8:00. Two or three breaks are enough; too many make viewers leave.

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)

The animals move gently and look around curiously, mouths closed. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward her face. ## Voice-over – full fortune video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Comment your sign now. By the end of this video, you'll know your lucky color, best time, and direction for today.

Today is a Dragon day. Coming up: one Great Day sign, two Good Days, one sign to take it easy, and all twelve.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
14 November belongs to the Dragon. Year of the Dragon, this Great Day is yours! A Yang Water day!

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)

សូម subscribe និងតាមដាន MyBirthSign រៀងរាល់ថ្ងៃ។ តើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំអ្វី? ប្រាប់ខ្ញុំក្នុងមតិយោបល់ផង! ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

## Keep the voice the same in every scene
Flow makes the voice fresh for every clip, so the voice text in every scene is written to describe exactly the same voice: the same words, in the same order, at the start of the audio line, for every scene and every video. To keep it that way:
- Always use "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Do not retype, shorten or translate the voice text, and do not add other words about the voice.
- Make all clips of one video in the same Flow project, with the same model and settings for every clip.
- Use the same language for the whole video (all English or all Khmer).
- Listen to each clip as it is made. If one sounds different, make only that clip again with the same copied prompt.

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Photorealistic live-action fantasy film, the look of a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with the grandeur of a Chinese historical fantasy drama. Real human faces with natural skin texture, the zodiac characters saved in Flow, each named after its animal (@Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog, @Pig): a guardian warrior in ornate armor themed on the animal, with a lifelike animal beside them (realistic anatomy, real fur, feathers or scales), always exactly as in the saved character picture, noble and powerful (never cute toys or cartoons). Setting: a majestic Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with towering carved stone, an enormous luminous full moon, deep indigo starry sky, drifting mist, a still reflecting pool with floating pink lotus flowers and ornate golden lanterns. A living, magical sky in every shot: thin silver clouds drift slowly across the moon, stars twinkle, a soft halo pulses around the moon and sky lanterns rise gently in the distance. Action and magic in the style of a Chinese fantasy (xianxia) drama: graceful wire-fu flying leaps, slow-motion martial-arts moves, flowing robes and capes, glowing sword energy and swirling qi light, always without blood, injury or fighting between characters. Golden magic: shimmering gold particles, flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light and floating gold dust that never hide a face. Physically real lighting: cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, soft lantern glow, volumetric light rays; colors deep navy, imperial gold, ruby red and lotus pink. Cinematic depth of field, smooth slow camera movement, high-end visual effects, 4K detail. The zodiac guardians and their animals never talk and never move their mouths; only Chanda, the presenter, speaks. No text, no letters, no subtitles, no watermark, no logos, no cartoon or plastic look, no low-quality CGI.

## Characters (already saved in Google Flow)
Your characters are saved in Flow as @Chanda and the 12 zodiac characters named after their animals: @Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog and @Pig. For each scene, add Chanda and the characters the prompt names as ingredients; the prompts below point to them with @ (for example @Snake), so keep the names in Flow exactly the same.

### Character reference image prompts
- **Chanda (ចន្ទា), the presenter (make once, use in every video):** Create a photorealistic portrait from a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with a Chinese historical fantasy drama: @Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists. Realistic human proportions, natural facial expression with a gentle closed-lip smile, real silk that folds and catches the light, real gold jewelry with true metallic reflections. Graceful, upright posture, one hand held open toward the temple as if presenting it, the other resting at her waist. She stands barefoot on a carved sandstone temple terrace with an ornate stone balustrade, beside a reflecting pool with pink lotus flowers; behind her, a temple inspired by Angkor Wat glows under an enormous luminous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, with golden lanterns and soft drifting gold dust. ## Scene 1 – Dragon day (Chanda on camera)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. She speaks with heartfelt enthusiasm: a joyful smile, bright eyes and lively, natural hand gestures, as if she can't wait to tell the viewer something special. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda. Then: magical and powerful. @Dragon (the Dragon guardian with their dragon) stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it uncoils and soars up around the temple towers as its guardian rides the wind behind it standing on a flying sword, both spiralling through clouds of golden light while a glowing pearl lights up the sky, as today's Water magic swirls around them: flowing ribbons of clear blue water, rippling rings of light and cool silver-blue mist. The lotus pond ripples with golden light, and lanterns rise into the sky. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. @Dragon (the Dragon guardian with their dragon), @Rat (the Rat guardian with their rat) and @Monkey (the Monkey guardian with their monkey) fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, a ring of golden energy explodes outward, lotus flowers bloom across the pond and a shower of sparkles and gold coins falls from the sky. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Dog, take it easy
Photorealistic golden fantasy-epic style, same saved Flow characters (Chanda and the zodiac characters named after their animals). @Dog (the Dog guardian with their dog) rests on a lotus pedestal by the pond: it rests quietly as a few fireflies keep watch around it, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The dog is the saved Flow character @Dog.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. Hundreds of sky lanterns rise from behind the temple and form a great glowing circle around the full moon, and the huge full moon glows over the pond. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with the twelve golden-armored zodiac guardians around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)
